HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B003","root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1ebf8ded9351b37188f4","text":"3. **Provisional morning.** Evidence: ض ح و B003 (forenoon meal and early-day grazing), ع ط و B002 in 93:5 (giving), ع ي ل B001/B002 in 93:8 (poverty; dependents), ن ع م B005 in 93:11 (grazing livestock). Structural cue: morning is later matched by giving, poverty-removal, and blessing. Mechanism: the packet supplies early grazing/meal imagery and a later economy of lack and sufficiency; I supply the coherence that morning light also opens a feeding/provision cycle. Abductive move: the cosmic sign is materially pastoral as well as optical. Reading change: \"duha\" can be read as the start of daily provision for exposed dependents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002","root_001692/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3061f6e00c609f0e3b3e","text":"2. **The same light exposes vulnerability and makes care visible.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ح و:B002` includes open exposure to sun and visible prominence; 93:6 supplies orphaned exposure through `ي ت م:B001` and shelter through `ء و ي:B001`. **Structural cue:** open daylight is followed at the window edge by a found vulnerable person being gathered into refuge. **Assigned role:** exposure names the risk; shelter answers it; visibility makes the answer observable. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state that daylight causes vulnerability; I infer only that the exposure/shelter contrast lets one image diagnose the other. **Reading change:** `ٱلضُّحَىٰ` is not brightness alone: it is the open field in which both need and protective response become manifest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B003","root_001028/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_413990de52e648f31b90","text":"3. **Exploratory: forenoon may carry a provision rhythm.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ح و:B003` is the forenoon meal and early-day grazing; 93:5 gives `ع ط و:B002`, handing over a gift, and 93:6 presents a dependent in need of shelter. **Structural cue:** the ordinary provision-time stands beside promised giving and dependent care. **Assigned role:** the meal/grazing branch supplies a recurring material cadence rather than a one-time spectacle. **Abductive move:** I infer that care of a dependent includes recurring sustenance; this is the weak link because the packet names giving and shelter but no food. **Reading change:** on this exploratory reading, the oath also evokes daily replenishment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002","root_000904/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4bc96dbeb5bef0ab999a","text":"93:6 supplied the strongest surprise: orphaned exposure followed by `فَـَٔاوَىٰ` activated the risk side of `ض ح و:B002`, so daylight became the place where vulnerability as well as rescue is visible. The gift in 93:5 and dependent care in 93:6 support `ض ح و:B003`, but because neither ayah names food, the provision reading remains exploratory rather than corrective.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_85d0cfb07aba1da44cd5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:1:1\",\"root_000904\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kuşluğun yemek ve otlatma vakti oluşu, sonraki verme ve muhtaçları gözetme dizisiyle günlük rızık ritmini çağrıştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002","root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a9fccf6504e535134ec0","text":"93:10 complicated the \"exposure\" reading: ض ح و B002 initially looked simply positive, but ن ه ر B002/B004 showed that public/daylight encounter can become harsh glare if it meets the asker with rebuke. 93:11 also strengthened the provision reading: ن ع م B005 and ح د ث B003 made morning not only light but the beginning of a distributable, narratable economy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aba21c39666f66b0eb1c","text":"2. **Open exposure before public narration.** Evidence: ض ح و B002 (open exposure to sun, public visibility), س م و B008 in 93:0 (good repute spread), ح د ث B003/B006 in 93:11 (report/conversation; disclosure). Structural cue: the surah begins with visibility and ends with commanded speech. Mechanism: the packet supplies exposed brightness and final narration; I supply the arrow from visibility to communicability. Abductive move: what is placed under open light becomes fit to be told rather than hidden. Reading change: the oath by duha becomes a summons into open acknowledgment, not merely a scenic image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_afd4394d0f8426212c6a","text":"4. **Sacrificial redistribution, exploratory.** Evidence: ض ح و B004 (Adha sacrifice), ه د ي B005 in 93:7 (offering sent to sanctuary), ن ع م B005 in 93:11 (livestock), س ء ل B001/B002 in 93:10 (asking/requested need). Structural cue: sacrifice, gift, livestock, and requester form a local ritual-gift field. Mechanism: the packet supplies branches for sacrifice and offering but the fixed word itself is simply \"duha\"; I supply a ritual redistribution arrow from consecrated animal to social need. Abductive move: the sacrificial branch is dormant but becomes thinkable because later commands regulate vulnerable recipients. Reading change: this exploratory reading lets 93:1 gesture toward a ritual economy in which brightness/provision is converted into giving.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b50438468730894e1a8d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:1:1\",\"root_000904\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açık gün ışığı, hem korunmasızlığı hem de ona verilen koruyucu cevabı görünür kılan bir alan haline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B002","root_000904/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b8c91b95db0addc6b9f4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:1:1\",\"root_000904\",[\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Açığa çıkaran ışık, surenin sonunda nimetin saklanmayıp anlatılmasına da zemin hazırlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d7823826dd947565a27e","text":"1. **Daylight is an extending phase, not a flash.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ح و:B001` is forenoon daylight rising and extending through the day. **Structural cue:** 93:2 places settled night beside it; 93:3 denies abandonment; 93:4–5 move toward a better later phase and future giving. **Assigned role:** extended daylight models care becoming progressively legible over time. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the temporal contrast and later sequence; I infer that increasing light maps onto increasing visibility of continuity. **Reading change:** the oath points to a process of disclosure in which a dark interval cannot by itself prove abandonment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_da7c99f5d8bd9e8abf3b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:1:1\",\"root_000904\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kuşluk, bir anda çakan ışığı değil giderek genişleyen bir aydınlanmayı çağrıştırır; ancak bu bağ doğrudan kurulmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fea8730cec49c473b182","text":"1. **Forenoon as delay that is not abandonment.** Evidence: ض ح و B001 (forenoon daylight extending; action delayed to that time), و د ع B001/B002 in 93:3 (leaving; restful ease), ء خ ر B002 in 93:4 (deferring to a later time). Structural cue: the first fixed image is a time after dawn, followed by settled night and then negation of abandonment. Mechanism: the packet supplies delayed daylight and later explicit deferral; I supply the causal inference that waiting until the risen part of day can be care rather than neglect. Abductive move: temporal lateness is reclassified by the surah as ripening.
